═══════════════════════ Figure 2a: OVB Bias Scatter ═══════════════════════\\n\\ndef generate_ovb_scatter_data(N=50, I=100, J=8, rho=0.6, n_seeds=20):\\n    all_c_true = []\\n    all_bias = []\\n    for seed in range(n_seeds):\\n        print(f\\\"    seed {seed}/{n_seeds}\\\", flush=True)\\n        data = generate_gated_mfrm_data(N=N, I=I, J=J, rho=rho, seed=seed)\\n        theta_true = all_bias.extend(bias.tolist())\\n    return np.array(all_c_true), np.array(all_bias)\\n\\n\\ndef plot_fig2_ovb_bias():\\n    print(\\\"Generating fig2_ovb_bias...\\\")\\n    all_c, all_bias = ax.axhline(0, color=\\\"gray\\\", linestyle=\\\"--\\\", linewidth=0.8, alpha=0.6)\\n\\n    z = np.polyfit(all_c, all_bias, 1)\\n    x_line = np.linspace(all_c.min(), all_c.max(), 100)\\n  fig.tight_layout()\\n    save_fig(fig, \\\"fig2_ovb_bias\\\")\\n    plt.close(fig)\\n\\n\\n# ════════��══════════════ Figure 4: Bias vs Rho ═══════════════════════\\n\\ndef    \\\"Bias Slope\\\"),\\n        (\\\"rmse\\\", r\\\"RMSE ($\\\\theta$ recovery)\\\", \\\"RMSE\\\"),\\n        (\\\"kendall_tau\\\", r\\\"Kendall $\\\\tau$\\\", \\\"Ranking Accuracy\\\"),\\n    ]\\n\\n    for ax, (col, ylabel, title) in zip(axes, metrics):\\n        for method in [\\\"sequential\\\", \\\"joint\\\"]:\\n            sub = df[df[\\\"method\\\"] == method]\\n            g = ax.plot(x, y, marker=MARKERS[method], color=COLORS[method],\\n                    label=method.capitalize(), linewidth=1.8, markersize=5)\\n            ax.fill_between(x, y - ci, y + ci, color=COLORS[method], alpha=0.12)\\n\\n        if col == \\\"bias_slope\\\":\\n            ax.axhline(0, color=\\\"gray\\\", ls=\\\"--\\\", alpha=0.5, linewidth=0.8)\\n\\n        ax.set_xlabel(r\\\"Confounding strength ($\\\\rho$)\\\")\\n        ax.set_ylabel(ylabel)\\n        ax.legend(framealpha=0.9)\\n        add_grid(ax)\\n\\n    fig.tight_layout()\\n    save_fig(fig,
